【葛洲壩航運擴能工程進入主體施工階段】三峽水運新通道工程是“十五五”時期我國開工建設的首個重大標誌性工程，包含三峽樞紐新通道和葛洲壩航運擴能。今天，葛洲壩航運擴能迎來關鍵節點，上下游圍堰正式開工建設，這標誌著葛洲壩航運擴能全面進入主體施工階段。上下游圍堰是葛洲壩航運擴能建設的第一道關卡，就是修築在江中的“臨時擋水牆”。其中，上游圍堰全長835.94米，下游圍堰總長960.85米。

### 2026-10-10 16:34:55  #國際 #原創

【土耳其禁止15歲以下兒童使用社交媒體賬號】根據土耳其官方公報釋出的新規定，土耳其已禁止社交媒體平臺向15歲以下兒童提供服務。社交媒體公司將被要求在使用者建立賬號時，透過土耳其電子政務系統使用年齡驗證令牌來驗證使用者的年齡。新規規定，一經查實使用者未滿 15 週歲，平臺必須遮蔽其賬號申請。該規定將於11月1日生效，此前土耳其議會於4月透過了一項法案。現有賬戶的年齡驗證預計將於2027年3月31日前完成，所有被發現未滿15歲使用者的賬號都將被關閉。根據新規，15至17歲使用者的賬戶預設設定為私密賬戶，並啟用內容過濾功能，且不提供個性化廣告或直播功能。平臺還需採取技術措施，防止使用者擷取這些賬號的螢幕截圖。平臺還必須為15至17歲使用者的賬戶提供家長控制工具，允許家長管理設定、限制使用時間並授權付費交易。根據土耳其網際網路法，不遵守規定的社交媒體平臺可能面臨高達其全球收入3%的罰款、廣告禁令以及網際網路頻寬限制。

### 2026-10-10 16:20:20  #宏觀 #公司 #市場

【未經批准含有“金融控股”等字樣 三家企業被要求整改】西藏金融監管局日前釋出公示，金融是特許經營行業，必須持牌經營。根據《國務院關於實施金融控股公司准入管理的決定》（國發〔2020〕4號）等有關規定，未經批准不得登記為金融控股公司，不得在公司名稱中使用“金融控股”“金融集團”“金控”等字樣。為嚴把金融准入關口，維護金融市場秩序，強化對非法金融活動的源頭管控，按照全國統一部署要求，西藏金融監管局會同市場監管部門對轄內涉金融經營主體進行全面摸排，經綜合研判。認定西藏轄記憶體在3家名稱未經批准含有“金融控股”“金融集團”“金控”等字樣的經營主體，分別是：1.拉薩經濟技術開發區亦興金控投資有限公司；2.西藏金控資訊服務有限公司；3.西藏金控基金管理有限公司。西藏金融監管局要求三家企業自公示之日起3個月內主動向市場監管部門申請登出或變更名稱、業務範圍，變更過後的名稱、經營範圍不得包含“金融控股”“金融集團”“金控”和其他未經許可的涉金融屬性字樣。對名單中沒有按期主動登出或變更名稱、業務範圍的經營主體，西藏金融監管局將聯合各相關部門採取進一步監管措施。 (央視新聞)

### 2026-10-10 16:01:22  #宏觀 #公司

【市場監管總局附條件批准分眾傳媒收購新潮傳媒股權案】10月10日，市場監管總局釋出公告，附加限制性條件批准分眾傳媒資訊科技股份有限公司（以下簡稱分眾傳媒）收購成都新潮傳媒集團股份有限公司（以下簡稱新潮傳媒）股權案。該案是我國廣告業領域第一起以附加限制性條件批准的經營者集中案件。本案的附條件批准，是反壟斷執法在廣告傳媒這一重要民生服務領域的進一步深化，也體現了市場監管總局對傳統服務行業競爭秩序的高度關注。經審查，市場監管總局認為該案對中國境內電梯媒體廣告市場可能具有排除、限制競爭效果。為有效減少此項經營者集中可能產生的不利影響，市場監管總局經過全面審查、科學論證，依法對該案作出附條件批准決定，要求分眾傳媒、新潮傳媒和集中後實體作出六項限制性承諾：繼續履行現有客戶合同及其所有商業條款；繼續保持對客戶的服務水平；集中後實體各城市的年度實際成交價不得超過生效日前二十四個月的分眾傳媒和新潮傳媒各自的對應城市實際成交價；以公平、合理、無歧視的原則繼續提供電梯媒體廣告服務，不得拒絕向客戶提供電梯媒體廣告服務、不得以搭售或附加不合理交易條件等方式限制客戶的自由選擇權；放棄已訂立、生效的獨家條款相關權利，對於新籤或續簽點位，不得達成獨家條款和自動續約條款；應進一步完善反壟斷合規制度，建立健全反壟斷合規管理體系。經評估，市場監管總局認為該承諾方案可有效減少本集中導致的競爭影響，能夠保障下游廣告主和消費者利益，維護相關市場公平競爭秩序，依法於10月9日附加限制性條件批准本案。市場監管總局將嚴格督促交易雙方有效履行限制性承諾，充分發揮經營者集中審查事前預防作用，切實維護我國電梯媒體廣告市場公平、創新、有序的競爭生態。

【Bundesbank official: risk of European debt crisis has risen sharply】Bundesbank Executive Board member Michael Theurer said the recent sovereign bond sell-off is a "clear warning signal" that investors are nervous about governments' ability to control debt. He said fiscal and political uncertainty is now affecting government bond markets; while there is currently no systemic sovereign debt crisis, risks have risen materially. French bonds plunged amid mounting concern over France's political and economic challenges, and the turmoil has spread to other highly indebted euro-area countries, prompting speculation the ECB may have to intervene. Echoing the IMF, Theurer called for a medium-term strategy to review spending and refocus outlays on growth-supporting investment. He added Germany's fiscal position remains stronger than most peers but rising debt means urgent consolidation is necessary.

【渣打銀行：中國股債大類資產重回均衡，股票為核心配置】渣打銀行釋出中國市場10月展望稱，當前中國資產更為均衡，體現在三個方面，一是中國大類資產重回均衡，三季度以來股票市場的修正，使得股票和債券重新回到相對平衡水平；二是成長/價值從之前相對高估回到合理水平，即超額收益從顯著偏離盈利增速差迴歸正常；三是投資者需要在產業投資和穩定紅利風格之間做好再平衡。同時，考慮到中國利率下行空間有限，但期限利差仍有收窄空間，可以透過拉長久期的方式增厚債券類投資收益。此外，該行將中國股票設定為核心配置。儘管中國在岸股票估值高於歷史平均，但在岸股票股權風險溢價重回歷史（自2011年起）中位數以上，即股票相對債券資產並未顯著高估。建議以紅利風格打底，產業投資追求長期回報，超配行業為資訊科技、通訊服務、能源、原材料；股票機會型包括AI產業鏈；有色金屬；新型電力系統；債券機會型為30年期國債。（21世紀經濟報道）

【二季度毛利率環比一季度下滑的原因是什麼？博遷新材回應】近日，博遷新材（605376.SH）釋出投資者關係活動記錄表。其中提到，2026 年二季度毛利率環比下滑主要受兩方面因素影響：一是生產成本升高，二季度新建產能陸續投產，人員招聘力度加大，新員工操作熟練度尚需提升，疊加裝置轉固後折舊費用計提入賬，導致單位生產成本有所增加；二是產品結構變化，高階 MLCC 需求旺盛，但受日韓廠商產能優先配置 AI訂單及高階產能擴張週期較長等因素影響，供給增長相對有限，因此對高階鎳粉增量採購有限，高階鎳粉整體需求趨於平穩，中高容值消費級MLCC 加速向中國大陸及臺灣地區轉移，放量產品以中端及中高階鎳基產品為主，毛利率相對較低。

【證監會山西監管局：對關愷採取出具警示函措施】中國證券監督管理委員會山西監管局官網公佈關於對關愷採取出具警示函措施的決定：經查，我局發現中財期貨有限公司山西分公司存在以下違規事項：一、績效及費用管理不規範，反映出分公司內部控制不健全，違反了《期貨公司監督管理辦法》（證監會令第155號，下同）第五十六條的規定。二、對大額虧損客戶風險提示不到位，反映出分公司風險管理存在薄弱環節，違反了《期貨公司監督管理辦法》第五十一條的規定。你作為分公司負責人，未按照《期貨公司董事、監事和高階管理人員任職管理辦法》（證監會令第227號，下同）第四條的規定切實履行職責，對上述問題負有管理責任。根據《期貨公司董事、監事和高階管理人員任職管理辦法》第二條第三款和第四十四條第一項的規定，我局決定對你採取出具警示函的監督管理措施，並記入證券期貨市場誠信檔案。如果對本監督管理措施不服，可以在收到本決定書之日起60日內向中國證券監督管理委員會提出行政複議申請，也可以在收到本決定書之日起6個月內向有管轄權的人民法院提起訴訟。複議與訴訟期間，上述監督管理措施不停止執行。

【中泰證券：維持芒果超媒“增持”評級，多維支援AI中長劇落地】中泰證券日前研報指出，芒果超媒AI多模態技術迭代加速AI中長劇商業化程序，改善線上影片、電視大屏生態可期。考慮到公司網際網路影片業務收入逐步企穩，毛利率雖短期受內容投入影響，有所承壓，但伴隨AI 中長劇落地有望迎來改善。可比公司方面，選擇行業內同樣從事網際網路影片業務的公司作為可比物件，包括愛奇藝、Netflix、嗶哩嗶哩。參考同業可比公司，考慮到公司作為稀缺的國有屬性線上影片平臺，主業逐步企穩，且積極推進AI中長劇佈局，有望提升盈利能力，開啟業績成長空間，給予估值溢價具備合理性，建議持續關注，維持“增持”評級。

### 2026-10-10 14:10:28  #Trading Economics #Economy #Egypt #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Egypt Inflation Rate at 7-Month Low of 13.9% — Egypt's annual urban inflation eased for the second straight month to 13.9% in September 2026 from 14.5% in August. It was the lowest inflation rate since February, as the impact of higher fuel prices continued to fade, with transport inflation slowing slightly to 24.3% in September from 24.4% in August, while housing and utilities inflation moderated to 35.2% from 42.8%. Egypt raised fuel prices in early March by about 14% to 17% across a wide range of petroleum products, marking the first increase this year amid elevated global oil prices. Inflation also moderated for clothing and footwear (13.9% vs 14.0% in August), health (4.5% vs 4.6%), and alcoholic beverages and narcotics (8.3% vs 9.1%). Meanwhile, inflation accelerated for food and beverages (7.2% vs 6.3% in August) and furnishings (17.4% vs 16.9%). On a monthly basis, the CPI rose 1.3%, after edging up 0.1% in the previous month, marking the fastest increase in four months.

### 2026-10-10 13:55:44  #宏觀

【國家發展改革委有關負責同志就《關於發展新質生產力的意見》答記者問】問：科技創新是發展新質生產力的核心要素。《意見》部署“大力推進科技創新”5項重大任務時有什麼考慮？

答：科技創新能夠催生新產業、新模式、新動能，是發展新質生產力的核心要素。《意見》將“大力推進科技創新”作為5大任務之首，就是為了強調其重要性。這一部分按照科技創新“幹什麼”、“誰來幹”、“幹成什麼樣”的邏輯，安排了5項重大任務。

一是從著眼長遠和立足當前兩方面部署“幹什麼”。著眼長遠，《意見》部署“加強原創性顛覆性科技創新”。主要考慮是，當前新一輪科技革命和產業變革加速突破，國際科技競爭向基礎前沿前移，迫切需要我們加強基礎研究，從源頭和底層解決關鍵技術問題，為新質生產力發展提供基礎理論支撐和源頭技術供給。立足當前，《意見》部署“加快突破關鍵核心技術”。主要考慮是，關鍵核心技術要不來、買不來、討不來，只有把關鍵核心技術掌握在自己手中，才能從根本上保障國家經濟安全、國防安全和其他安全。

二是從有為政府和有效市場相結合部署“誰來幹”。從政府有為角度，《意見》部署“統籌國家戰略科技力量建設”。主要考慮是，世界科技強國競爭，比拼的是國家戰略科技力量。這些年，我們發揮新型舉國體制優勢，在體系化攻關上積累了不少經驗。《意見》總結成功經驗，從體制機制改革、重大科技專案、重大創新平臺等方面，對建強國家戰略科技力量作出部署，目的就是要增強科技創新體系化攻關能力。從市場有效角度，《意見》部署“強化企業科技創新主體地位”。未來產業興起、新興產業發展、傳統產業升級改造，都要發揮市場配置創新資源的決定性作用，都要靠企業一步一步突破帶動。只有強化企業科技創新主體地位，才能破解科技和經濟“兩張皮”問題，提高國家創新體系的整體效能。

三是從現實生產力目標部署幹成什麼樣。習近平總書記強調，創新不是發表論文、申請到專利就大功告成了，創新必須落實到創造新的增長點上，把創新成果變成實實在在的產業活動；要圍繞發展新質生產力佈局產業鏈，推動短板產業補鏈、優勢產業延鏈、傳統產業升鏈、新興產業建鏈。為貫徹落實習近平總書記重要論述，《意見》將“加速科技成果向現實生產力轉化”作為重大任務之一，在“大力推進科技創新”中部署，就是要引導大家更加重視應用導向，將科技創新目標錨定在發展新質生產力上。

---

### 2026-10-10 13:54:36  #其他

【國家發展改革委有關負責同志就《關於發展新質生產力的意見》答記者問】問：《意見》為什麼要突出強調全面實施“人工智慧+”行動？

答：《意見》在“推動科技創新和產業創新深度融合”部分，主要部署“改造提升傳統產業”、“培育壯大新興產業”、“前瞻佈局未來產業”、“促進實體經濟和數字經濟深度融合”等內容。突出強調“全面實施‘人工智慧+’行動”，主要有3方面考慮。

一是人工智慧是數字經濟時代引領新一輪科技革命和產業變革的戰略性技術。當前，全球人工智慧技術加速迭代演進，加快發展新一代人工智慧成為事關我國能否抓住新一輪科技革命和產業變革機遇的戰略問題。《意見》在現代化產業體系建設部分，突出強調全面實施“人工智慧+”行動，是為了發揮我國產業體系完備、市場規模巨大、應用場景豐富等優勢，以實際應用加速人工智慧技術不斷向上生長，形成以創新帶應用、以應用促創新的良性迴圈。

二是人工智慧是發展新質生產力、建設現代化產業體系的核心支撐。人工智慧正從實驗室走向生產生活，催生出無人駕駛、人形機器人、自主飛行無人機、腦機介面、智慧手機、智慧電視、智慧電腦、智慧穿戴裝置等新領域新賽道，同時對賦能傳統產業升級也發揮著重要作用。《意見》突出強調全面實施“人工智慧+”行動，是為了推動人工智慧與實體經濟深度融合，助力傳統產業改造升級、新興產業培育壯大、未來產業前瞻佈局，及時將智慧領域創新成果應用到具體產業、具體產業鏈條上，以智慧化帶動產業高階化、綠色化，賦能千行百業轉型升級。

三是人工智慧技術突破和商業化規模化應用是一項長期的複雜系統工程。從科技突破看，要加強基礎理論研究和關鍵核心技術創新，構建自主可控、協同執行的基礎軟硬體系統。從要素支撐看，要統籌推進算力設施建設，深化資料資源開發利用和開放共享，加強人才引育。從規模應用看，要推動人工智慧同各行各業深度融合，構建創新活躍的智慧經濟和更有溫度的智慧社會。從安全保障看，要加緊制定完善相關法律法規、政策制度、應用規範、倫理準則，構建技術監測、風險預警、應急響應體系，確保人工智慧安全、可靠、可控。《意見》突出強調全面實施“人工智慧+”行動，是為了更好地實施這一複雜系統工程。

【如何看待未來幾年鋰價走勢？國城礦業回應】近日，國城礦業（000688.SZ）釋出投資者關係活動記錄表。投資者提問，如何看待未來幾年鋰價走勢？如何應對鋰價波動對金鑫礦業經營效益的影響？公司表示，上一輪鋰價上行主要由終端需求爆發（電動車）疊加產業鏈補庫驅動，價格彈性大但波動劇烈。本輪需求端除電動車外，儲能成為主力增量——電網側、大儲裝機持續放量，未來，AI和算力中心的建設帶來了新的用電和備電需求，資料中心配套儲能正在形成增量場景；機器人、低空經濟、消費電子這些新興應用也在逐步開啟。同時，這一輪供給端放量更分散、成本曲線更陡。因此，位於成本曲線左側、資源自給率高的企業優勢會更加突出，價格下行期足以穿越週期，價格上行期彈性增大；公司新能源業務依靠“資源自給+礦化一體”的成本結構，盈利彈性建立在成本線安全邊際範圍內，可以有效規避鋰價波動對金鑫礦業的經營效益影響。

【抑鬱焦慮有新療法 腦機介面能治精神疾病】過去，抑鬱症、焦慮症等問題的診斷和治療，主要依靠問診、量表和醫生經驗。但情緒問題的背後，往往隱藏著大腦功能的變化。記者探訪上海市精神衛生中心，這裡設有國內第一個精神科腦機介面與神經調控治療室。治療焦慮、抑鬱、失眠等常見的精神心理問題，這裡能夠做到“一人一方”，甚至有的患者不用吃藥。除了“聽患者怎麼說”，這裡的醫生還會藉助磁共振、腦電、近紅外等技術看一看患者“腦子裡發生了什麼”。找到存在問題的腦區後，醫生會進一步確定治療靶點，再有針對性地進行無創神經調控。目前，這項技術在抑鬱症領域應用較多，不過醫生也強調，它並不是人人都適用，也不能完全替代藥物治療和心理治療，而是為患者多提供了一種選擇。記者瞭解到，目前一次神經調控治療大約90元，相關檢查和治療費用中，部分專案已納入醫保。（央視財經）

【世界糧食計劃署司長：厄爾尼諾疊加戰爭影響，一場“慢燃式”糧食危機正在形成】聯合國世界糧食計劃署糧食安全與營養分析司司長讓-馬丁·鮑爾（Jean-Martin Bauer）在專訪中表示，世介面對的已不是一次孤立、短暫的糧食危機，而是一個不斷被戰爭、疫情、能源衝擊和極端天氣激化的結構性問題。“全球糧食體系比過去更容易受到系統性擾動，我們必須為一個此類危機將常態化的世界做好準備。”我們現在看到的是一場“慢燃式”危機。它首先影響能源和化肥，隨後逐漸傳導至農業生產。燃料和化肥成本上升對作物生產造成的影響，將進一步體現為糧食價格上漲。與此同時，一些地區還面對乾旱和降水異常，東非和中非部分地區降雨不足尤其令人擔憂。（澎湃新聞）

【Reserve Bank of India opens dedicated oil USD window, tightens FX rules to support rupee】The Reserve Bank of India will open a dedicated FX window on Monday to supply dollars to three state-owned oil companies, meeting their full daily USD needs via dollar sales through designated banks. RBI has barred re-registration of any previously cancelled rupee-related FX derivative contracts. It cut the threshold for hedges that do not require proof of underlying assets from an equivalent of $100m to $5m. RBI also introduced an FX risk reserve requiring banks to deposit cash equal to 20% of the rupee-equivalent nominal amount with the central bank for any transaction with nominal value above $2m.

### 2026-10-10 11:33:45  #MKT News

【SET revises short-selling and HFT rules】The Stock Exchange of Thailand will implement revised short-selling and high-frequency trading rules from Nov. 16. Stocks priced 5–50 THB will trade with reduced tick sizes to narrow bid-ask spreads and cut trading costs. Short selling will be restricted to higher-liquidity instruments: SET100 constituents, single-stock futures underlyings, depositary receipts and ETFs; short sales will be permitted only at or above the last traded price. The exchange will levy extra fees on accounts that submit large numbers of orders with relatively low execution rates. It will eliminate per-security dynamic price bands, relax restrictions on HFT participants’ eligible instruments, and remove minimum order resting-time requirements to improve order management. Trading hours for foreign-linked derivative warrants and ETFs will start at 08:00 (two hours earlier) from Nov. 16; closing times unchanged.

【China to implement new national freight timetable from Oct. 10 18:00】China State Railway Group will implement a new national freight train timetable from Oct. 10 at 18:00. 【公司是否有提價計劃？五糧液：不存在固定的價格比值關係】有投資者向五糧液（000858.SZ）提問，公司是否有提價計劃？茅臺零售價已經突破1800元/瓶了，五糧液現在800元，兩瓶五糧液還換不了一瓶茅臺，公司認為這個零售價比值是否合理？公司是否研究過短期內該合理比值應是多少？長期（五年以上）是否有將該比值提升的經營計劃？關於批發價和零售價的價格體系建設有何戰略規劃？價格體系、現代高效分銷渠道、品牌力提升是否統籌規劃？能否詳細介紹一下10月10日，公司回答表示，五糧液產品價格調整始終遵循“匹配五糧液品牌價值、順應市場供求關係、保障渠道健康”的原則。公司會密切關注行業的整體消費復甦態勢、終端動銷節奏以及供需結構變化，採取靈活、審慎的產品價格策略，綜合考量是否及何時對核心產品價格進行調整。不同品牌高階酒的終端市場價格受多重因素共同影響，不存在固定的價格比值關係。公司堅持價格體系維護、現代化高效分銷渠道構建與品牌力提升進行一體化統籌規劃、協同推進：一是深度推進營銷體制改革，堅持透過營銷數字化管理“按需投放、精準調控”。二是公司持續最佳化“傳統渠道+創新渠道”雙輪驅動模式。三是公司圍繞“和美文化”主線，透過持續打造“文化五糧液”，全面提升高階消費群體的品牌認同感。

Key measures: an 'e‑commerce + industrial cluster' cultivation program and encouragement for local e‑commerce product‑selection centers; targeted support for SMEs with core technologies and original designs to connect to e‑commerce channels and raise brand recognition; use of platform channels such as order direct procurement and foreign‑trade premium product zones to help export firms expand in the domestic market and grow consumption of premium export goods. 【China's basic pension insurance participants reach 1.079 billion at end-September】The Ministry of Human Resources and Social Security said at a State Council briefing that as of end-September participants in China’s basic pension, unemployment and work-injury insurance stood at 1.079 billion, 251 million and 327 million respectively, and the three social insurance funds had a cumulative surplus of 11.2 trillion yuan. The ministry said it will encourage and support flexible-employment and new-form workers to join employee pension insurance, expand enterprise annuity pilots, advance implementation of the personal pension system, improve social-security transfer and continuation rules, promote nationwide interoperability of social-security services and a unified social-security card, and strengthen supervision to safeguard and preserve and increase fund value.

【截至9月底 我國基本養老保險參保人數達10.79億人】國務院新聞辦公室今天（10月10日）上午舉行“開局起步‘十五五’”系列主題新聞釋出會，人力資源社會保障部相關負責人介紹推進就業和社會保障高質量發展有關情況。在社會保障方面，截至9月底，全國基本養老保險、失業保險、工傷保險（含職業傷害保障）參保人數分別達到10.79億人、2.51億人、3.27億人，三項社會保險基金累計結餘11.2萬億元。下一步，我國將鼓勵支援靈活就業、新就業形態人員參加職工養老保險，積極開展擴大企業年金覆蓋面試點，紮實推進個人養老金制度實施，完善社保關係轉移接續政策，積極推進更多社保服務事項全國通辦和社保卡一卡通用，加強社保基金安全監管和保值增值，讓社會保障更實在、更最佳化、更可靠。（央視新聞）

### 2026-10-10 09:04:38  #宏觀

【《團體標準化 良好行為規範》國標釋出】市場監管總局批准釋出《團體標準化 良好行為規範》國家標準，該標準規定了團體標準組織開展標準化活動的總體要求、職能管理和制度要求，以及團體標準制定程式、團體標準起草、團體標準實施應用、國際化等方面的要求，確立了團體標準化良好行為評價的指標體系和評價規則。自《中華人民共和國標準化法》確立團體標準法律地位以來，我國團體標準發展迅速，在快速響應市場需求、促進技術創新、推動產業升級、支撐新產品新業態新模式發展等方面發揮了積極作用。但部分團體標準仍存在多而不精、快而不優，部分社會團體開展團體標準化活動不規範等問題。該標準堅持問題導向，以合規引導為重點，將團體標準全生命週期管理要求融入其中。團體標準作為我國標準體系的重要組成部分，是市場自主制定標準的主要形式。該標準釋出實施，將有利於引導社會團體規範地開展團體標準化活動，提升團體標準供給質量。

今日12大單品的終端零售總價繼續回落，雖然漲跌平品種各佔四款，但跌幅相對突出，整體價盤延續弱勢。如果12大單品各取一瓶整體打包售賣，今日總售價為10719元，較昨日小幅下跌7元，連續第三日走低，創下18天以來新低。

【港股IPO新經濟企業募資超七成 發行人“年輕化”特徵很鮮明】2026年港股IPO（首次公開募資）市場迎來深刻的結構性變革，發行人整體“年輕化”成為鮮明的市場特徵。資料顯示，以上市日期為統計口徑，2026年以來，港股新增上市企業達116家，合計募資總額約3880億港元。本輪上市潮中，一批成立週期短、核心團隊年輕化、深耕前沿科創賽道的新銳企業集中登陸港交所，重新整理港股上市速度紀錄。其中，思格新能僅用時3年11個月完成掛牌，重新整理中國企業港股IPO的最快紀錄。MiniMax、天辰生物等科創企業，也均在6年內實現快速上市，成為港股IPO年輕化的典型代表。隨著新銳科創企業紛紛上市，近年來港股IPO發行人從成立至上市的平均年限持續縮短。業內人士分析指出，港股上市主體年輕化並非短期市場偶然現象，而是資本市場制度創新、國內產業升級等因素共振的結果。這一趨勢不僅逐漸重塑港股的行業格局與估值體系，更為市場開啟了新的發展空間與投資機遇。（證券時報）

【廣州土拍維持高熱度 央國企仍為拿地主力】今年以來，廣州優質居住地塊持續受到各大央企、地方國企、民企青睞，多宗地塊實現溢價成交。10月9日，廣州市黃埔區KXCGS-I4-8地塊迎來出讓，共吸引了保利發展、越秀地產、中海地產、廣開控股4家房企報名參拍。最終，保利發展以16.65億元的價格競得該地塊，摺合樓面價為每平方米21738元，溢價率為26.6%。就在9月30日，廣州海珠區琶洲南區HZ0305401-CZTB地塊競價熱度高漲，吸引了保利發展、越秀地產、珠實地產、廣州城投4家企業參與競拍。歷經41輪競價，該地塊以38.1687億元成交，溢價率為26.5%，成交樓面地價每平方米55385元，也由保利發展競得。中指研究院的資料顯示，今年前9個月300城住宅成交面積同比下降23%，出讓金下降21%。9月，300城住宅用地成交面積同比下降34%，出讓金同比下降20%。中指研究院研究主管陶淑茹表示，從全國市場來看，央國企仍為拿地主力，但拿地有所分化。保利發展、華潤置地、越秀地產拿地金額同比增長明顯，拿地銷售比也較高，投資拿地力度較大。今年前9個月，頭部房企拿地銷售比均值為48%，較前8個月增加1個百分點，拿地強度保持穩定。（證券時報）

【第三季度21家上市銀行獲511家機構調研】今年第三季度，證券公司、基金公司、保險公司、保險資管公司以及外資等各類機構密集對上市銀行進行調研。資料顯示，第三季度，共有21家上市銀行獲511家機構調研。調研內容顯示，信貸投放、淨息差、資產質量等話題備受關注。從調研情況來看，機構投資者對區域性銀行的關注度較高。其中，江蘇銀行最受機構青睞，共被146家機構調研；杭州銀行被73家機構調研；瑞豐銀行被64家機構調研；寧波銀行被63家機構調研；南京銀行被43家機構調研；紫金銀行被29家機構調研；廈門銀行被21家機構調研。天府立言金融研究院院長曾剛對記者表示，區域性銀行紮根地方經濟，與區域產業結構、民營小微企業、縣域經濟和居民財富密切相連，在經濟活躍、財政實力較強、產業叢集鮮明的地區，往往擁有較好的客戶基礎、較強的存貸款獲取能力及更高效的信貸投放渠道。此外，部分優質區域性銀行長期保持較快的營收、利潤及資產擴張速度，資產質量也相對穩健，形成了“規模增長—收益改善—風險可控”的經營特徵。（證券日報）

Ibovespa Hits Record High on Election Polls — The Ibovespa rose 1.4% to close at a record 209,067 on Friday as the first voting-intention polls released after the first round confirmed Flávio Bolsonaro’s lead over President Lula in the runoff. Markets view Bolsonaro as more fiscally restrictive, strengthening expectations that a potential administration could tighten control over public spending and improve Brazil’s fiscal position. The race remains tight, with some polls showing a statistical tie. Financial stocks were mostly higher, with Itaú up 2.3%, BB and Itaúsa gaining 1.6% each, and B3 advancing 2.5%. Petrobras rose 0.9% despite volatile oil prices. Vale turned positive late in the session, closing up 0.38% in a narrow range after iron ore prices hit multi-month lows. Retailers posted sharp gains as yields fell following the polls, despite rising inflation and a reading above the upper limit of the BCB’s target range. Magazine Luiza soared 19.2%. The Ibovespa gained 8.82% this week, its strongest weekly performance of 2026.

Lundin surged 10.5% after its Fruta del Norte mine in Ecuador posted its strongest production quarter on record. Energy producers also gained despite volatile oil prices. The TSX rose 0.9% weekly.

【US Treasury eases Russia sanctions, allows imports of Russian diesel until April 2027】The US Treasury's Office of Foreign Assets Control (OFAC) on Oct. 9 issued General License No. 135 permitting transactions previously banned under Russia sanctions involving Russian-origin diesel — including sale, delivery, discharge and import into the United States. Brent Wavers on Potential Russian Diesel Release — Brent crude prices wavered near $104 a barrel on Friday after US President Trump said Russian President Putin had agreed to release diesel supplies into the global market. The US Treasury Department said the Office of Foreign Assets Control was “immediately issuing a temporary general license” to allow Russian diesel supplies to reach global markets. The proposed volume would amount to about 22.5 million barrels. Meanwhile, the US-Iran conflict has strained shipping, with a large tanker fleet tied up in ship-to-ship transfers outside the Strait of Hormuz, pushing global freight rates to record highs and reducing vessel availability on other routes. Eleven tankers came under attack while transiting Hormuz during the week that ended October 4th. Trump said earlier this week that he would not strike Iran again before the November midterm elections, citing what he described as productive discussions with Tehran. On the week, brent rose about 1.5%.

【Hurricane Isaias forces shutdown of 72% of US Gulf crude output】As Category 3 Hurricane Isaias approached the US Gulf coast, producers shut about 1.46 mln b/d of crude — roughly 72% of regional output. The US Bureau of Ocean Energy Management said personnel were evacuated from 129 offshore platforms and two mobile drilling rigs were moved out of the storm path as of 1200 EDT Friday. BP shut Thunder Horse and Na Kika and removed non‑essential staff from three additional platforms. The outages pushed Gulf differentials wider: Mars crude traded about $3/bbl over WTI and Thunder Horse crude carried a premium of more than $4/bbl for a second straight session.

### 2026-10-10 03:36:27  #Trading Economics #Markets #Mexico #Currency #Importance 1

Mexican Peso Weakens Toward One-Year Low — The Mexican peso weakened to around 18.44 per US dollar in October, nearing a one-year low after the release of the Bank of Mexico’s meeting minutes. The currency became one of the worst performers among emerging markets as the minutes left open the possibility of further interest-rate cuts. Most members of the Governing Board said moderating core inflation and weak domestic consumption provide room to continue easing monetary policy into late 2026 and early 2027. Expectations of a narrowing interest-rate differential between Mexico and the US reduced the peso’s appeal in carry trades, boosting demand for dollars in the spot market. Meanwhile, annual inflation rose to 3.45% in September from 3.26% in August. Although slightly below market expectations, the reading added uncertainty to the domestic monetary policy outlook.

### 2026-10-10 03:22:46  #Trading Economics #Markets #Brazil #Government Bond 10Y #Importance 1

Brazil 10-Year Yield Falls to Two-Year Low — Brazil’s 10-year government bond yield fell to around 12.68% in October, reaching a two-year low amid a rally in domestic assets driven by the election outlook. The first voting-intention polls released after the first round confirmed Senator Flávio Bolsonaro’s lead over President Lula in the runoff scheduled for October 25th, although some results remained within a statistical tie. Markets view Bolsonaro as more fiscally restrictive, and his lead strengthened expectations that a potential administration could tighten control over public spending and take steps to improve Brazil’s fiscal position. Lower pressure on public finances could reduce the risk premium demanded by investors, while tighter fiscal policy could ease inflationary pressures, potentially allowing the Central Bank of Brazil to cut interest rates more quickly.

### 2026-10-10 03:13:43  #Trading Economics #Markets #Brazil #Currency #Importance 1

Brazilian Real Strengthens on Election Runoff Polls — The Brazilian real strengthened to below 5.00 per US dollar on Friday following the release of the first polls for the presidential runoff. A Datafolha poll showed Senator Flávio Bolsonaro with 49% of voting intentions against 45% for President Lula, a statistical tie at the limit of the survey’s two-percentage-point margin of error. An Atlas/Bloomberg poll put Flávio at 51.1%, compared with 45.7% for Lula, ending the statistical tie in the previous survey’s runoff scenario. Markets view Bolsonaro as more fiscally restrictive amid elevated domestic yields and weak business activity, and his economic team is considering spending cuts equivalent to around 2% of GDP. Flávio finished ahead of Lula in the first round, defying most polls and sending the real to a 17-month high on Monday. Meanwhile, the BCB held a regular foreign-exchange swap rollover auction on Friday. Such interventions are used to help reduce exchange-rate volatility during periods of political and economic uncertainty.

【知名經濟學家克魯格曼警告法國或引發歐洲債務危機】諾貝爾經濟學獎得主保羅·克魯格曼警告稱，法國不斷惡化的公共財政正走在一條“不可持續的道路”上，有可能引發一場席捲歐元區的債務危機。克魯格曼在10月8日釋出的一篇文章中寫道：“法國目前的狀況確實令人擔憂。尤其是這並不只是法國自己的問題。法國作為歐元區成員可能爆發一場失控的債務危機，而這樣的危機可能會破壞歐洲團結。”克魯格曼指出：“法國可能已經跨過了從‘大到不能倒’到‘大到救不起’的界線。如果法國危機達到最糟糕的程度，將令歐洲內部出現嚴重分裂。”克魯格曼表示，根據信用違約掉期(CDS)定價，市場價格反映的法國違約機率只有區區1.2%，但他認為這一機率“太低”。“法國政府債券當前CDS價格突然上升，非常令人擔憂。”

### 2026-10-10 00:43:57  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Cotton Holds Near Two-Month Low — Cotton futures fell below 81 cents per pound but remained near a two-month low amid prospects of rising global supply. The latest WASDE report raised its global production forecast for the August 2026–July 2027 season to 25.8 million tonnes from 25.66 million previously, driven by higher projections for the US (2.93 million vs. 2.88 million tonnes) and Brazil (4.15 million vs. 4.03 million tonnes). Export competition is also expected to intensify, with Brazil’s export forecast raised to 3.48 million tonnes from 3.38 million. Shipments from Brazil between July and September of 2026 increased 18% from a year earlier to 453 thousand tonnes. Meanwhile, US exports have declined in recent weeks, though forecasts remain unchanged at 2.69 million tonnes between August 2026 and July 2027. Adding to the bearish pressure, 23% of the US crop has been harvested, above the historical average of 21%. However, a hurricane threatening cotton crops in Alabama and the Delta could pose risks to supply.

DAX Finishes Higher, Still Posts Weekly Loss — Frankfurt's DAX 40 gained ground to close about 1.1% firmer at 25,102 on Friday, reversing a two-day decline that sent the index to the lowest in two months. Investor sentiment improved after President Trump ruled out a US attack on Iran before the November midterm elections, citing progress in talks to end the conflict. His remarks pushed oil prices lower and eased pressure on the bond market. Meanwhile, caution prevailed amid persistent tensions in the Gulf. Software giant SAP was the top performer, rising 3.8%, followed by Zalando (3.2%) and Bayer (3.2%). Siemens, BMW, Deutsche Post and Adidas, Fresenius Medical Care and Siemens Energy also advanced firmly, each adding over 2%. On the downside, Deutsche Telekom plunged nearly 8% as SpaceX’s spectrum acquisition raised concerns over increased competition for T-Mobile US, in which it holds a 54% stake. For the week, the index lost about 0.5%.

### 2026-10-10 00:07:25  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Brent Rises Past $103 — Brent crude inched rose past $103 per barrel on Friday, the highest in two weeks as mounting supply risks counterbalanced temporary relief that the US would not escalate the conflict against Iran this month. Freight costs for tankers crossing the Persian Gulf soared to record highs, and traffic through the Strait of Hormuz declined as maritime agencies flagged a series of attacks on vessels in the area. Ongoing conflict across the Middle East continued to disrupt OPEC+ output, curtailing crude flows to major refining hubs. Adding to supply concerns, recent EIA data showed that the US Strategic Petroleum Reserve fell to a near-record low. Meanwhile, Hurricane Isaias forced operators in the Gulf of Mexico to shut in over 60% of the region's crude production capacity. These disruptions overshadowed the brief pullback triggered when US President Trump indicated that Iran would not face strikes before the midterm elections on November 3rd.

FTSE 100 Closes at Over 1-Week High — The UK's FTSE 100 rose further to close about 1.1% up at 10,552 on Friday, its highest since September 30, halting a two-day losing streak. Sentiment improved as oil prices retreated, easing pressure on bond yields, after Trump signalled a pause in potential US military action against Iran ahead of next month's midterm elections and described ongoing talks as “productive”. Software-related stocks were among the best performers, with Relx, Sage Group and Experian rising 4.8%, 4.3% and 4.2%, respectively. Mining stocks also advanced strongly, with Antofagasta surging over 5%, amid firmer prices of copper. Fresnillo rose more than 3%, tracking gains in silver and gold. On the flip side, telecom stocks including Airtel Africa (-6.3%), Vodafone (-4.9%) and BT Group (-2.4%) faced pressure after SpaceX agreed to acquire spectrum to strengthen Starlink Mobile’s US ambitions. For the week, the FTSE 100 rose by 0.9%.

### 2026-10-09 23:17:05  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Rice Futures at Over 2-Year High — Rice futures have sustained their recent gains, trading near $17 per hundredweight, their highest level since July 2024, amid concerns over tightening supply and rising logistical costs. In its October outlook, the FAO forecast global rice production at 552.5 million tonnes for the 2026/27 season, down 2.4% from the prior season’s record. Although global stocks are expected to remain historically high, potentially cushioning supply pressures, adverse weather is adding to production risks in key Asian markets. In India, output is forecast to fall by 6.5% in 2026 to 144 million tonnes, as below-normal monsoon rainfall and reduced summer planting weigh on production. Meanwhile, flooding in Thailand is disrupting river transport and delaying planting for the next crop, while the risk of El Niño-related weather disruptions adds further uncertainty over yields across Asia. Higher fuel, freight and insurance costs amid geopolitical tensions in the Middle East are also supporting prices.

【Capital Economics: Odds of a Canadian October rate hike fall sharply; expects rate normalization only next year】Capital Economics economist Thomas Ryan said that after two consecutive months of sizable employment declines and a small rise in the unemployment rate, only a September inflation print well above expectations would put an October BoC rate hike back on the table. Canadian employment fell 68,300 in September, far below consensus for a modest increase and below Capital Economics’ prior forecast of no change. Ryan said the labour market is not as weak as the aggregate data suggest: part of the drop reflects a pullback among younger workers after unusually strong summer hiring, and weakness is concentrated in the public sector. He still expects the BoC to wait until 2027 to begin normalizing policy rates.

【Fed survey: Nearly 20% of U.S. households report delinquencies; debt-service burden highest since 2013】The Federal Reserve's latest consumer finances survey shows rising U.S. household debt stress. Median debt-service-to-income ratio climbed to 15.4% (up 2ppt vs 2022); the Fed cited higher mortgage and consumer loan rates as a likely driver. The share of households with debt-service ratios at or above 40% rose to 8.6% from 6.5%, the highest since 2013. Households reporting loan delinquencies rose to nearly 20% in 2025 from about 12% in 2022, underscoring worsening repayment strain as borrowing costs have increased.

【再生鋁產品出口暴增160%】再生鋁，作為原鋁的重要替代原料，今年以來市場熱度持續走高。央視財經記者走訪發現，在浙江台州，再生鋁市場貨源緊俏，優質廢鋁一料難求。採購商駐場蹲守搶貨：“優質廢鋁太緊缺，只要有好貨進場，我們基本全部拿下，就怕斷料停工。”再生鋁產品出口高速增長，低碳優勢開啟海外市場空間。資料顯示，生產一噸原生鋁，碳排放可達16噸二氧化碳當量，生產一噸再生鋁僅約0.5噸二氧化碳當量，相比原生鋁碳排放降幅95%左右。2026年1至8月，我國未鍛軋鋁及鋁材累計出口466.5萬噸，同比增長16.8%；其中以再生鋁合金錠為主的未鍛軋鋁合金累計出口34.2萬噸，同比增幅近85%，6月單月出口同比增長達160.6%，創下近年新高。（央視財經）

【U.S. consumer sentiment falls to 46.3 in early October; current conditions index hits record low; inflation expectations rise】University of Michigan preliminary October consumer sentiment index fell to 46.3, the lowest since May, the survey showed on Friday. The current economic conditions index dropped to 44.7 from 50.9 in September, a record low. The expectations index rose to 47.3 from 46.3, its first uptick since July. One-year inflation expectations rose to 4.7% (from 4.6%); five- to ten-year expectations edged up to 3.5%, slightly above September. The survey cited high gasoline prices, rising borrowing costs and slowing hiring as pressures on household finances; survey director Joanne Hsu said the confidence decline was concentrated among low-income households and those with small equity portfolios.

TSX Rises on BoC Rate-Hold Expectations — The S&P/TSX Composite Index rose about 1% to near 35,500 on Friday as weaker employment data reinforced expectations that the Bank of Canada will refrain from hiking rates. Employment fell by 68,300, missing expectations for a 7,000 increase, while the unemployment rate edged up 0.1 percentage point to 6.5%, in line with forecasts. Canadian bond yields moved lower and oil prices eased from recent peaks, supporting credit-sensitive stocks. RBC, BMO, Scotiabank and CIBC rose around 0.5% each. Gold prices also advanced, lifting mining shares, with Agnico Eagle, Barrick and WPM gaining more than 2% each. Energy stocks rose as oil prices remained elevated, with Canadian Natural and Suncor up about 1%. Elsewhere, Aritzia surged nearly 15% after beating expectations for second-quarter revenue and profit.

New measures would extend beyond court staff to punish firms that provide services to the ICC, potentially disrupting the court's operations. Germany said it remains committed to equal partnership with France and that cooperation will focus on future armoured‑combat technologies. ### 2026-10-09 22:05:25  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #Consumer Confidence #Importance 3

US Consumer Sentiment Falls to Five-Month Low — The University of Michigan’s consumer sentiment index fell to 46.3 in early October 2026, according to the preliminary estimate, its lowest level since May’s record low and well below market expectations of 47.6. While year-ahead expectations for personal finances and business conditions edged up slightly, buying conditions for durable goods deteriorated sharply amid elevated prices and borrowing costs. Lower-income consumers and households with smaller stock portfolios recorded particularly steep declines, reflecting their limited capacity to absorb further price increases. Frustration over the cost of living continues to mount, with consumers across the political spectrum increasingly perceiving a deterioration in the economic outlook since the beginning of the year. Meanwhile, inflation expectations edged higher, with year-ahead expectations rising to 4.7% from 4.6% in September, while long-run inflation expectations increased to 3.5% from 3.4%.

【商務部新聞發言人就美國召集部分經濟體釋出應對所謂“結構性產能和生產過剩”問題部長級聯合宣告答記者問】商務部新聞發言人就美國召集部分經濟體釋出應對所謂“結構性產能和生產過剩”問題部長級聯合宣告答記者問。問：美東時間10月7日，美貿易代表辦公室（USTR）釋出公告稱，近期美國召集14個經濟體高階官員舉行會議，共同簽署釋出應對所謂“結構性產能和生產過剩”問題的部長級聯合宣告。請問商務部對此有何評論？答：我們注意到有關情況。中方已在不同場合多次闡明關於所謂“產能過剩”問題的看法，並於今年7月專門釋出了有關立場檔案。中方一貫認為，看待產能問題要秉持全面、客觀、公正的態度，不能出於對自身產業競爭力和市場地位的擔憂，將經貿問題政治化。中方反對個別國家以所謂“產能過剩”為名，行貿易保護主義之實，這隻會擾亂全球經貿秩序和產供鏈穩定，給世界經濟增長帶來長期風險。如果未來有關經濟體以此為藉口，採取損害中方利益的措施，中方將作出必要回應。中方堅持在開放合作中建設現代化產業體系，支援構建公正合理的國際經濟秩序。中方呼籲有關各方堅持多邊主義，倡導互利共贏，尊重市場規律，共同維護開放包容的全球產供鏈合作格局。

【美債收益率走高令歐洲經濟多角度承壓】近期，美債收益率持續走高，連續擾動歐洲金融市場。10月8日，美國10年期國債收益率盤中升至約5.37%，創24年來新高。受其影響，歐洲多國國債收益率近期也在上升：英國10年期國債收益率升至5.527%，創2007年以來最高水平；法國10年期國債收益率升至4.961%，甚至一度觸及近24年來高位。分析認為，美債收益率持續高企使得歐洲國家政府融資成本上升，加重財政負擔；跨市場資產價格波動可能放大金融風險，美元走強則可能推高能源和原材料進口成本，增加通脹壓力。若上述因素疊加，歐洲央行在抑制通脹與支援經濟增長之間將面臨更大政策權衡，歐洲經濟復甦也可能承受額外壓力。（新華社）

### 2026-10-09 21:44:12  #Trading Economics #Markets #Canada #Currency #Importance 1

Canadian Dollar Extends Losses — The Canadian dollar weakened slightly to around 1.43 per US dollar in October, extending a 12-week low as weaker employment data reinforced expectations that the Bank of Canada will hold interest rates steady. Employment fell by 68,000, missing expectations for a 7,000 increase, while the unemployment rate edged up 0.1 percentage point to 6.5%, in line with forecasts. Recent data also pointed to a cooling economy. Real GDP rose 0.2% in August, as gains in mining and quarrying and retail trade partly offset a decline in oil and gas extraction. GDP was essentially unchanged in July, ending a three-month growth streak. Although August’s result matched expectations, it highlighted a weaker start to the third quarter. Meanwhile, uncertainty over US-Canada trade ties persisted after US President Donald Trump said Canada had been difficult to deal with, casting doubt over the prospects for a new trade deal.

Canada 10-Year Yield Retreats as Employment Declines — Canada’s 10-year government bond yield fell to around 3.9% after reaching a three-year high of over 4% on October 1st, as weaker employment data reinforced expectations that the Bank of Canada will hold interest rates steady. Employment fell by 68,000, missing expectations for a 7,000 increase, while the unemployment rate edged up 0.1 percentage point to 6.5%, in line with forecasts. Recent data also pointed to a cooling economy. Real GDP estimates rose 0.2% in August, as gains in mining and quarrying and retail trade partly offset a decline in oil and gas extraction. GDP was essentially unchanged in July, ending a three-month growth streak. Although August’s result matched expectations, it highlighted a weaker start to the third quarter. Meanwhile, elevated oil prices continued to fuel inflation concerns and expectations of further rate hikes by central banks, keeping global bond markets under pressure. The US 10-year Treasury yield remained near multi-decade highs.

Ibovespa Rises Following Election Runoff Polls — The Ibovespa rose about 1% to near 208,000 on Friday following the release of the first polls for the presidential runoff. A Datafolha poll showed Senator Flávio Bolsonaro with 49% of voting intentions against 45% for President Lula, a statistical tie at the limit of the survey’s two-percentage-point margin of error. An Atlas/Bloomberg poll put Flávio ahead at 51.1%, compared with 45.7% for Lula, ending the statistical tie in the previous survey’s runoff scenario. Markets view Bolsonaro as more fiscally restrictive amid elevated domestic yields and weak business activity. His economic team is considering spending cuts equivalent to around 2% of GDP. Financials posted solid gains, with Banco do Brasil up nearly 2%, Bradesco and Itaú rising more than 1% and B3 adding about 2.5%. Utilities also advanced, with Axia up more than 1% and Sabesp gaining about 2%. Vale edged higher as iron ore prices stabilized after reaching multi-month lows amid weakening steel margins.

US Stocks Rise on Friday — US stocks moved higher on Friday, with the S&P 500 gaining 0.4%, the Nasdaq rising 0.6% and the Dow Jones advancing 107 points. The gains were driven by a rebound in tech shares following the previous session’s sell-off. Sentiment was supported by reports that OpenAI expects to reach or exceed $70 billion in annualized revenue by year-end, easing concerns that sales were falling short ofprojections. Meanwhile, lower oil prices helped alleviate some pressure on the bond market. Consumer discretionary and real estate stocks were also among the strongest performers. Tesla jumped 2.7% as Elon Musk made it clear who will own and operate Terafab. On the other hand, Apple shares fell 2.5% after reports it told some suppliers to cut production of components for iPhone 18 Pro and Pro Max models. Delta Air Lines was also down 3% after missing on earnings and cutting its outlook. On the week, both the Dow and the Nasdaq are little changed while the S&P 500 is up 0.6% so far.

They reviewed progress on four workstreams—trade and investment balance, export controls, intellectual property and WTO reform—and held reciprocal discussions on major economic and trade issues. RAIMONDO raised concern over recent EU trade-restrictive measures, urging the EU to treat China as a partner in resolving disputes, pursue dialogue consistent with WTO rules and mutual interests, and seek new cooperation to support global growth and supply-chain stability. ### 2026-10-09 21:28:58  #宏觀 #國際

【商務部部長王文濤與歐委會貿易和經濟安全委員謝夫喬維奇共同召開中歐貿易投資磋商機制第二次例會】10月8-9日，商務部部長王文濤在北京與歐委會貿易和經濟安全委員謝夫喬維奇共同主持召開中歐貿易投資磋商機制第二次例會並發表聯合宣告。雙方立足“穩定、平衡的中歐關鍵貿易伙伴”定位，共同盤點了首次會議以來機制項下貿易和投資平衡、出口管制、智慧財產權、世貿組織改革四個工作板塊磋商進展，並就中歐經貿重要議題進行了對等、專業、建設性的討論。

雙方同意繼續就市場準入問題保持溝通磋商，逐步推動解決醫療器械、農食產品、逆變器融資禁令等彼此關切，持續推動中歐貿易向上平衡。雙方同意進一步強化中歐出口管制對話機制，繼續加強政策溝通，推動解決雙方個案關切，研究出臺便利化措施的可能性。雙方同意就歐盟外國補貼條例（FSR）開展技術對話，繼續就智慧財產權保護和世貿組織改革加強交流合作。雙方同意探討投資以及面向未來的合作。

王文濤就歐盟近期出臺的經貿限制措施表達關切，重申中國不是歐盟問題的根源，而是解決問題的夥伴；敦促歐方與中方相向而行，在符合世貿組織規則和照顧彼此利益的前提下，透過對話磋商妥處摩擦分歧，探索合作新機遇、拓展新空間，為促進國際經濟增長和全球產供鏈安全穩定貢獻中歐力量。

謝夫喬維奇表示，中國是歐盟重要經貿夥伴，願與中方在歐中貿易投資磋商機制項下，加強溝通交流，透過對話磋商管控摩擦分歧，共同尋找建設性的解決方案，推動歐中經貿關係更加平衡穩定發展。

雙方均認為，第二次例會是中歐深化合作的新起點，責成工作團隊持續推進後續工作，推動相關成果共識儘早落地，為中歐貿易投資磋商機制第三次例會做好準備。

【重慶商業投資集團有限公司原董事長範光明被開除黨籍】中央紀委國家監委網站10月9日通報，據重慶市紀委監委訊息：經重慶市委批准，重慶市紀委監委對重慶商業投資集團有限公司原黨委書記、董事長範光明嚴重違紀違法問題進行了立案審查調查。經查，範光明喪失理想信念，背棄初心使命，對黨不忠誠不老實，對抗組織審查，搞迷信活動；違背組織原則，不按規定報告個人有關事項，在職工錄用、工作調動中為他人謀取利益並收受財物；廉潔底線失守，違規收受禮金、消費卡，透過民間借貸獲取大額回報，違規從事營利活動；腐化墮落，利用職務便利為他人在工程承攬、資質審批、職工錄用等方面謀利，並非法收受鉅額財物。範光明嚴重違反黨的政治紀律、組織紀律和廉潔紀律，構成嚴重職務違法並涉嫌受賄犯罪，且在黨的十八大後不收斂、不收手，性質嚴重，影響惡劣，應予嚴肅處理。依據《中國共產黨紀律處分條例》《中華人民共和國監察法》《中華人民共和國公職人員政務處分法》等有關規定，經重慶市紀委常委會會議研究，決定給予範光明開除黨籍處分；按規定取消其退休待遇；收繳其違紀違法所得；將其涉嫌犯罪問題移送檢察機關依法審查起訴，所涉財物一併移送。

【斯洛伐克稱美國理解其進口俄羅斯石油和天然氣的必要性】斯洛伐克經濟部長德尼薩·薩科娃表示，美國對斯洛伐克在2027年底前繼續進口俄羅斯石油和天然氣的必要性表達了“絕對理解”。薩科娃稱，斯洛伐克正努力擺脫對莫斯科化石燃料的依賴，但在明年繼續接收俄羅斯石油時，該國需要獲得潛在美國製裁的豁免。與此同時，根據歐盟的全面禁令，斯洛伐克必須在2027年秋季前停止進口俄羅斯天然氣。薩科娃週五在布拉迪斯拉發告訴記者：“美方對我們的處境表達了絕對理解，也理解我們希望實現斯洛伐克供應多元化的時間表。我們至少需要這個過渡期持續到2027年底。”本週，薩科娃在華盛頓與白宮國家能源主導委員會執行董事賈羅德·阿根以及美國財政部副部長弗朗西斯·布魯克舉行了會談。為替代俄羅斯天然氣工業股份公司的供應，斯洛伐克計劃簽訂一份每年約10億立方米的天然氣中短期或長期合同。薩科娃表示，阿爾及利亞、亞塞拜然和美國都是潛在的供應國，這些供應量約佔國有天然氣供應商斯洛伐克天然氣工業公司所需總量的三分之一。

【Canada Sept payrolls fall 68.3k; unemployment 6.5%; YTD job gains erased】Canada payrolls fell 68.3k in September, well above expectations, erasing earlier year-to-date employment gains, Statistics Canada said. The unemployment rate rose to 6.5% from 6.4%. Losses were concentrated in the public sector (–70k), led by education (–35.3k) and health and social assistance (–23.1k); manufacturing fell 12.7k. Since December 2025 total employment is down 41k, with the past two months down 110k. Labour force participation declined 0.2 ppt to 64.8%, the lowest since December 1997 excluding 2020; Statistics Canada largely attributed the drop to population aging.

【四川省林業和草原局原黨組成員、副局長賓軍宜嚴重違紀違法被開除黨籍】四川省紀委監委9日通報，日前，經四川省委批准，四川省紀委監委對四川省林業和草原局原黨組成員、副局長賓軍宜嚴重違紀違法問題進行了立案審查調查。 經查，賓軍宜理想信念喪失，紀法意識淡薄，對抗組織審查；違反組織原則，利用職權為他人謀取人事利益；為官不廉，違規收受禮金，違規從事營利活動；私德不修；貪慾膨脹，利用職務上的便利以及職權或者地位形成的便利條件，為他人謀取利益並收受財物，數額特別巨大。 賓軍宜嚴重違反黨的政治紀律、組織紀律、廉潔紀律和生活紀律，構成嚴重職務違法並涉嫌受賄犯罪，且在黨的十八大後不收斂、不收手，性質惡劣，情節嚴重，應予嚴肅處理。依據《中國共產黨紀律處分條例》《中華人民共和國監察法》《中華人民共和國公職人員政務處分法》等有關規定，經四川省紀委常委會會議研究並報四川省委批准，決定給予賓軍宜開除黨籍處分；按規定取消其享受的待遇；收繳其違紀違法所得；將其涉嫌犯罪問題移送檢察機關依法審查起訴，所涉財物一併移送。（新華社）

### 2026-10-09 20:41:54  #Trading Economics #Economy #Canada #Employment Change #Importance 2

Canada Employment Drops the Most in 7 Months — Employment in Canada declined by 68,300 in September 2026, the steepest decline in seven months and missing expectations for a 7,000 increase, up from a 41,700 decline in August. This marked the second consecutive monthly decline, with both full-time and part-time employment falling by 0.2% and 0.9%, respectively. Public sector employment dropped by 70,000, extending its decline to a fourth consecutive month, while private sector employment was little changed for the second month in a row. Self-employment also edged down by 23,000. Youth employment fell by 48,000, marking its second consecutive monthly decline. By industry, employment decreased in educational services (-2.2%), health care and social assistance (-0.8%), and manufacturing (-0.7%). By province, the largest declines were recorded in Quebec (-1.1%), British Columbia (-0.7%), and Manitoba (-0.6%). The employment rate edged down to 60.6% from 60.8%. On an annual basis, employment was up by 95,000 or 0.5%.

### 2026-10-09 20:35:25  #Trading Economics #Economy #Canada #Unemployment Rate #Importance 1

Canada Unemployment Rate Inches Up as Expected — The unemployment rate in Canada inched up to 6.5% in September 2026 from a two-year low of 6.4% in the previous month, in line with market expectations. The jobless rate remained below its two-year average, when the Canadian economy slowed under the Bank of Canada's aggressive rate-hiking cycle and elevated inflation. The number of unemployed people rose by 15,400 to 1.475 million during the period, even as the labor force participation rate fell by 0.2 percentage points to 64.8%, its lowest level since 1997 when excluding the initial shock of the COVID-19 pandemic. The unemployment rate rose by 0.3 pp to 5.3% for core-aged women, offsetting the 0.2 pp reduction to 5.8% in that for men. The rise in the headline rate coincided with an unexpected 68,300 drop in net employment, contrasting with expectations of a slight increase.

### 2026-10-09 20:22:55  #Trading Economics #Economy #Brazil #Inflation Rate #Importance 2

Brazil Inflation Edges Above Forecasts — Brazil’s annual inflation rate rose to 4.58% in September 2026 from 4.22% in August, slightly above forecasts of 4.50%. Price growth accelerated for food and beverages (4.66% vs. 3.53%), household articles (2.08% vs. 1.40%), clothing (3.34% vs. 3.25%), transport (3.94% vs. 3.04%) and communication (2.79% vs. 1.96%). Transport costs were partly driven by vehicle fuel inflation, which rose to 4.97% from 4.41% as international oil prices surged amid Middle East tensions. Meanwhile, price growth moderated for housing (4.22% vs. 4.90%), health and personal care (5.75% vs. 5.84%), personal expenses (5.90% vs. 6.16%) and education (5.95% vs. 5.98%). Month over month, consumer prices rose 0.82%, rebounding from a 0.32% decline in August. Inflation reached 3.95% year to date.

### 2026-10-09 20:18:58  #國際

【我國牽頭制定的船舶壓載水管理系統除錯測試國際標準釋出】記者今天瞭解到，國際標準化組織（ISO）近日釋出《船舶與海洋技術 壓載水管理系統（BWMS）電解法壓載水管理系統的除錯和測試程式》國際標準。該標準由中國牽頭，美國、德國、加拿大、瑞士、挪威、丹麥等國專家參與制定。船舶壓載水攜帶的外來生物是海洋生物入侵的重要途徑之一，嚴重威脅海洋生態安全。壓載水管理系統是殺滅這些入侵生物的關鍵裝置，其殺滅效果取決於產品本身設計效能及安裝後現場除錯水平。該標準規範了電解法壓載水管理系統除錯測試技術要求，規定系統執行時長不少於60分鐘、排放取樣體積不少於1立方米，並對大於等於50微米‌和10微米至50微米‌兩類生物進行達標驗證，確保滿足國際公約排放標準，為全球船東、船廠、裝置製造商和行業主管部門提供統一的、科學的壓載水管理系統除錯測試程式和驗收依據。截至目前，我國牽頭制定3項壓載水管理系統相關國際標準，覆蓋系統設計、除錯等關鍵環節，為遏制有害水生物透過壓載水跨海域傳播發揮重要作用，助力全球海洋生態環境保護。（央視新聞）

South Africa 10-Year Bond Yield Stays Near 6-Month Highs — South Africa’s 10-year government bond yield hovered around 9%, near its highest level since late March, as investors continued to monitor developments in the Middle East and their implications on the inflation outlook. Although President Trump signalled progress in talks with Iran, concrete steps towards de-escalation are still needed, while risks to shipping through the Strait of Hormuz persist. In South Africa, petrol and diesel prices climbed to unprecedented levels following the latest adjustment on october 7, surpassing the all-time highs recorded in June and further complicating the central bank's goal to bring inflation back to its 3% target. The South African Reserve Bank (SARB) recently warned that the risk of second-round inflation effects had increased amid persistently elevated oil prices and uncertainty surrounding the impact of El Niño. The repo rate was raised by 25 bps to 7.25% in September, with analysts pencilling in two further hikes over the next six months.

【江蘇官宣推出詞元（Token）券，最高補貼500萬元】江蘇五部門近日聯合印發支援人工智慧創新發展有關實施方案的通知，集中出臺一批政策支援搶跑智慧經濟新賽道，其中就包括《江蘇省詞元（Token）券實施方案》。該方案由江蘇省發改委、省財政廳、省資料局聯合出臺。所謂“詞元券”，其實就是一筆財政補貼資金。企業、高校院所、研究機構及OPC等主體購買智慧算力服務（含智慧算力租賃）進行模型訓練、模型推理、智慧體應用等的，都有機會申領這筆補貼。那麼能補多少錢？方案明確，省市支援總額原則上不超過實際支付費用的50%。單個主體支援上限不超過500萬元。（微訊江蘇）

【南非期待進一步深化對華合作】南非官員8日在2026年南非國家品牌論壇上說，期待進一步深化對華合作，歡迎中國企業擴大對南投資及更多中國遊客赴南旅遊。南非總統府副部長肯尼斯·莫羅隆接受新華社記者採訪時說，南非努力為全球投資者營造具有確定性和公信力的政策環境，堅持對外開放。南非期待與中國合作伙伴在投資、貿易、旅遊及人文等領域持續開展務實合作，進一步鞏固和深化兩國友好關係，實現互利共贏。論壇主辦方“品牌南非”組織執行長內維爾·馬奇高度贊同莫羅隆的表態。他說，南非是通往非洲大陸的天然門戶，熱切邀請中國企業來此設立區域總部，進而拓展更廣泛的非洲大陸市場。得益於南非日益最佳化的營商環境，大批中國企業已在南非紮根發展，雙方建立起堅實的合作互信。“品牌南非”組織是南非官方國家形象推廣機構，舉辦此次論壇旨在凝聚企業、社會組織及公眾力量，共同維護和提升南非的全球形象。據瞭解，近年來，中南經貿合作穩步推進，中國企業積極深耕南非的可再生能源、綠色技術、礦業及汽車製造等關鍵領域。（新華社）

【貝森特聘請朱迪·謝爾頓擔任顧問，後者曾被特朗普提名進入美聯儲理事會但引發巨大爭議】朱迪·謝爾頓的經濟觀點極具爭議，當年參議院否決了她的美聯儲理事提名；如今她獲聘擔任美國財政部長斯科特·貝森特的顧問。 美財政部表示，謝爾頓將為貝森特提供匯率政策方面的諮詢。美財政部將於本週五晚些時候釋出公告稱：“在職業生涯中，她長期專注分析各國國內貨幣與金融形勢，以及這些因素對匯率產生的影響。”此番任命出爐之際，財政部高層人事正處於動盪階段。媒體近期一篇報道統計，截至8月底，已有7名經參議院確認的財政部官員離職，目前僅補位了其中一個崗位。 貝森特近期任命傑富瑞前首席市場策略師戴維·澤爾沃斯擔任顧問，與謝爾頓頭銜相同。謝爾頓與澤爾沃斯二人的任命均無需參議院批准。

【華維設計：因資訊披露違規等，江西證監局對公司及相關責任人出具警示函】華維設計10月9日公告，公司於近日收到中國證券監督管理委員會江西監管局下發的《行政監管措施決定書——關於對華維設計集團股份有限公司、廖宜勤、侯昌星採取出具警示函措施的決定》（〔2026〕17號），經查，公司2025年年度報告中淨利潤資料披露不準確、主要客戶及供應商披露不準確，臨時公告中關於收購標的公司九江華維芯微電子有限公司車間面積披露有誤。上述行為違反了《上市公司資訊披露管理辦法》的相關規定，董事長廖宜勤、時任董事會秘書兼財務總監侯昌星未能忠實、勤勉地履行職責，對上述違規行為負有主要責任。根據相關規定，江西證監局決定對公司、廖宜勤、侯昌星採取出具警示函的監管措施，並記入證券期貨市場誠信檔案。

### 2026-10-09 19:27:08  #Trading Economics #Economy #Ukraine #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Ukraine Inflation Climbs to 11-Month High — Ukraine’s annual inflation rate rose to 10% in September 2026, the highest level since October 2025, up from 8.1% in the previous month. Transport inflation accelerated to 26.9% from 23.8% in August, while inflation also accelerated sharply for housing and utilities to 6.3% from 5.6%, and for food to 8.9% from 6%. Price growth also picked up for furnishings (7.3% vs. 5.7%). Meanwhile, inflation in restaurants and hotels eased to 13.2% from 13.7%, while deflation in clothing and footwear moderated slightly to 4.7% from 4.9%. On a monthly basis, consumer prices surged 2.1%, the largest increase since June 2024, following a 0.1% rise in August.

### 2026-10-09 18:25:25  #Trading Economics #Economy #Portugal #Balance of Trade #Importance 1

Portugal Trade Deficit Narrows in August as Exports Outpace Imports — Portugal’s trade deficit narrowed to €2.99 billion in August 2026, from €3.11 billion a year earlier. Exports increased 6.8% to €5.36 billion, driven by a 61.7% surge in fuels and lubricants, amid higher prices. Exports also rose for machinery and other capital goods (16.7%) and industrial supplies (6.5%), while transport equipment fell 19.1%, mainly due to lower passenger-car sales. Exports to the Netherlands rose 46.8% and to Spain 11.2%, while shipments to the US fell 28.5%. Imports increased just 2.8% to €8.35 billion, led by a 23.5% rise in fuels and lubricants and a 16.7% increase in machinery and other capital goods. Imports of industrial supplies declined 8.6%, mainly due to chemicals. On the other hand, imports rose from China (27.3%) and Spain (8.1%), but plunged from Ireland (75.9%). For the first eight months of 2026, however, Portugal’s trade deficit widened to €24.42 billion, from €21.97 billion a year earlier.

Earlier threats of US duties prompted traders to redirect shipments to American warehouses, contributing to the recent rally in prices. Near-term purchasing was also expected in China as manufacturers returned from holidays with low inventories.

### 2026-10-09 17:45:50  #Trading Economics #Markets #South Africa #Currency #Importance 0

South African Rand Remains Subdued — The South African rand traded around 16.5 per US dollar, near its lowest level since July, as a firm US dollar and elevated crude prices continued to weigh on the currency despite a recent pullback in oil prices. Meanwhile, gains in key precious metals, including gold, provided only limited support. Locally, higher fuel costs have intensified inflationary pressures, increasing the risk of further monetary tightening. The South African Reserve Bank (SARB) recently warned that second-round effects could cause higher fuel and transport costs to feed through to broader prices, wages and inflation expectations. The risk of a severe El Niño event have added to upside risks, particularly for imported and food inflation. The SARB argued that early action was necessary to prevent temporary price shocks from becoming entrenched, despite weak growth. Policymakers raised the rates by 25 bps to 7.25% last month, while markets are pricing in two more increases over the next six months.

Malta Industrial Production at 4-Month High — Malta’s annual industrial production rose by 5.1% year-on-year in August 2026, following an upwardly revised 4.0% increase in the previous month. This marked the highest reading since April, driven by higher output in the manufacturing sector (2.9% vs 2.6% in July), particularly in the production of wood, paper products, and printing (44.9%), other manufacturing (8.1%), and computer, electronic and optical products, and motor vehicles (3.5%). By industrial grouping, output also increased for energy (33.9% vs 23%), while it recovered for consumer durable goods (0.4% vs -1.1%) and intermediate goods (3.5% vs -3.2%). On a seasonally adjusted monthly basis, industrial production edged up 0.1% in August, down from a revised 3.2% increase in July, marking the smallest gain since May.

Mongolia Trade Surplus Narrows to Lowest in a Year — Mongolia’s trade surplus narrowed to USD 444.8 million in September 2026, its lowest level since August 2025, down from USD 487.5 million in the same month a year earlier. ### 2026-10-09 16:01:56  #Trading Economics #Markets #United Kingdom #Stock Market #Importance 1

UK Stocks Recover — The UK's FTSE 100 rose 0.9% on Friday, halting a two-day decline, led by software and mining stocks, while softer oil prices and some stabilization in bond yields calmed investors. Trump's remarks that the US would not attack Iran before next month's midterm elections, describing talks with Tehran as "productive", helped ease geopolitical concerns. Sage Group and RELX led the gains, rising 4% and 3.2%, respectively. Mining stocks also advanced strongly, with Antofagasta and Fresnillo gaining more than 3% each, supported by firm prices of copper, silver and gold. Tesco (+2.8%) gained following positive reactions from Deutsche Bank and Jefferies to its interim results, published a day earlier, which showed a 6.5% rise in profits and an improved annual outlook. Vodafone Group and Airtel Africa were the laggards, falling 3.8% and 3.4%, respectively, mirroring the broader sector decline across the European telecom sector. For the week, the index was set to gain about 0.7%.

### 2026-10-09 15:57:29  #Trading Economics #Markets #France #Stock Market #Importance 0

French Stocks Set for Second Weekly Loss — The CAC 40 rose 0.8% to 7,791 on Friday, recovering from two consecutive sessions of losses as geopolitical concerns subsided after President Donald Trump dismissed the prospect of a US strike on Iran ahead of the midterm elections. Trump also said Washington was “having productive discussions” with Tehran, pushing oil prices lower and easing pressure on bond yields. Adding to the positive sentiment, markets pared back expectations for ECB rate hikes, with money markets pricing in the deposit rate rising from 2.50% currently to 2.72% by December and 3.20% by late 2027. Domestically, French markets have faced mounting pressure as investors weigh the country’s debt burden, fiscal deficit and political uncertainty ahead of the 2027 presidential election. Among the notable gainers were L’Oréal (1.3%), Schneider Electric (1.4%), Hermès (2.1%) and Safran (1.2%). For the week, the CAC 40 remained on track to post its second consecutive weekly decline.

### 2026-10-09 15:55:12  #Trading Economics #Markets #Germany #Government Bond 10Y #Importance 1

Bund Yields Retreat as Oil Prices Ease — Germany’s 10-year Bund yield eased to 3.45%, moving further away from a 17-year high touched in late September, supported by falling oil prices and safe-haven demand. Brent crude retreated from recent highs as concerns over Middle Eastern oil supplies eased after US President Donald Trump said Washington would not launch an attack on Iran before the November midterm congressional elections, citing productive talks aimed at ending the conflict. Markets also scaled back expectations for ECB tightening, with the deposit rate now priced to rise from 2.50% currently to 2.72% by December and 3.20% by late 2027. Before the recent widening in bond spreads, investors had anticipated roughly one additional rate hike in early 2027. Meanwhile, borrowing costs and risk premiums on French and Italian government debt declined after rising sharply last week, as investors reassessed fiscal and political risks across the euro area.

French Bond Yields Ease as ECB Rate Hike Bets Retreat — France’s 10-year OAT yield eased to 4.85%, retreating slightly from a more than two-decade high, as markets scaled back expectations for European Central Bank tightening following a stall in the oil rally after US President Donald Trump said Washington would not attack Iran before the November midterm elections. Money markets now price the ECB deposit rate rising from 2.50% currently to 2.72% by December and 3.20% by late 2027. Before the recent widening in bond spreads, investors had anticipated roughly one additional rate hike in early 2027. French bonds remain under pressure from expectations of higher ECB rates and political uncertainty ahead of the 2027 presidential election, which have heightened concerns over France’s ability to rein in its public finances. The spread between French OATs and German Bunds narrowed slightly this week to around 135 basis points, down from 159 bps last Friday, its widest level since November 2011.

Azerbaijan GDP Grows 1.5% in January–September — Azerbaijan’s economy grew 1.5% year-on-year in January–September 2026, following a 1.2% expansion in the first eight months of the year. The non-oil and gas sector continued to advance (2.6% vs 2.1% in January–August), while contraction in the oil and gas sector deepened (-1.2% vs -0.8%). Growth accelerated in transportation and warehousing services (10.0% vs 8.7%) and information and communication services (11.1% vs 10.7%). Meanwhile, growth moderated in agricultural output (1.1% vs 2.6%) and retail trade turnover (3.8% vs 3.9%), while industrial output continued to decline (-0.8% vs -0.4%). Investment in fixed capital rose 10.9%, compared with 10.0% previously, amid stronger growth in the non-oil and gas sector (3.1% vs 1.4%).

【財政部：2026年超長期特別國債上半年已發行5720億元，完成全年發行任務的44%】財政部發布2026年上半年中國財政政策執行情況報告，其中提到，國債管理不斷加強。 

一是積極做好國債發行工作。上半年發行國債7.75萬億元，其中記賬式國債75650億元、儲蓄國債1897億元。今年4月，啟動2026年超長期特別國債發行，上半年已發行5720億元，完成全年發行任務的44%，整體進度快於去年，有力保障重大專案順利推進。 

二是有序啟動2026年主權債券發行工作。合理設計主權外債幣種結構、期限結構、發行地及創新品種。今年5月，在香港成功發行60億元人民幣綠色主權債券，發行收益率創人民幣主權債券簿記建檔發行歷史最低水平，認購倍數為10.4倍，訂單規模重新整理離岸人民幣簿記建檔發行紀錄，主權類投資規模及佔比創新高。6月，在盧森堡成功發行50億歐元主權債券，創亞太區歐元債發行歷史最大規模、發行利差歷史最低等多項記錄。常態化發行香港人民幣國債，上半年累計發行香港人民幣國債505億元。 

三是順利推進儲蓄國債（電子式）納入個人養老金產品範圍落地實施。2026年6月，如期順利上線個人養老金儲蓄國債（電子式）業務，支援加快多層次多支柱養老保險體系發展，滿足投資者使用個人養老金購買儲蓄國債（電子式）的需求。 

四是持續加強儲蓄國債電子渠道建設。新增手機銀行渠道銷售成員，目前40家儲蓄國債承銷團成員已全部可透過手機銀行向投資者銷售儲蓄國債（電子式），人民群眾購債便利度進一步提高。同時，合理調整單人購買單期儲蓄國債限額，使儲蓄國債惠及更多群眾。 

五是推動國債納入中英櫃檯債業務並完成首筆交易。指導境內國債登記託管結算機構完善相關係統建設，為境外投資者在境內開立實名賬戶，在保障中英櫃檯國債業務順暢有序執行的同時，全面夯實跨境互聯業務的風險防範基礎。中國建設銀行正式將國債納入中英櫃檯債業務品種，並於6月1日與滙豐銀行完成首筆1億元人民幣國債交易。

### 2026-10-09 15:40:06  #宏觀

【財政部：截至7月末各地發行置換債券1.73萬億元 完成2026年2萬億元額度的86.7%】財政部發布2026年上半年中國財政政策執行情況報告，其中提到，存量隱性債務風險有效緩釋。 

一是有序化解存量隱性債務。落實一攬子化債方案，指導各地紮實推進地方存量隱性債務化解工作。截至7月末，各地發行置換債券1.73萬億元，完成2026年2萬億元額度的86.7%。 

二是加快推動地方政府融資平臺改革轉型。指導地方加快化解融資平臺存量隱性債務，依法依規釐清政府和企業權責，加快剝離融資平臺政府融資功能。配合金融管理部門最佳化金融支援融資平臺債務風險化解政策，引導金融機構透過債務重組等方式，降低融資平臺流動性風險和利息負擔。 

專項債券管理最佳化完善。 

一是最佳化專項債券用途管理。新增專項債券重點支援重大專案建設、置換隱性債務、消化政府拖欠賬款等。堅持正向激勵原則，統籌考慮黨中央、國務院確定的重大戰略和重大專案支出需要，根據各地區債務風險、財力狀況、管理水平以及專案資金需求等情況合理分配額度，向專案準備充分、投資效率較高、資金使用效益好的地區傾斜。截至7月末，發行新增專項債券2.41萬億元，支援建設專案超1.8萬個。 

二是擴大“自審自發”試點範圍。經國務院批准，2026年將河北省、江西省、湖北省、重慶市納入新增“自審自發”試點範圍。指導督促新納入試點地區進一步完善工作機制，制定本地區試點工作方案，強化部門協同配合，提升專案儲備質量，加快發行使用進度。上半年，“自審自發”試點地區發行新增專項債券1.6萬億元，佔全國發行額的77%。 

三是強化專項債券專案稽核。指導督促各地健全專案常態化謀劃儲備機制，做深做實專案前期工作，推動儘快具備開工條件。指導督促“自審自發”試點地區完善稽核標準，加強對非“自審自發”試點地區專案稽核和指導把關，對無收益專案堅決禁止，對重複建設、投入產出比低的專案加強把關。 

四是完善專項債券全鏈條管理。指導督促各地統籌把握專項債券發行節奏和進度，做到早發行、早使用。加強專項債券資金穿透式監管，開展專項債券資金使用情況“掃描式”核查，對違規行為堅決查處。加強專項債券專案資產管理，建立專項債券專案資產臺賬，推動形成從資金管理到資產管理、從債務端到資產端的管理閉環。

【Ministry of Finance: As of end-July localities issued 1.73 trillion yuan of replacement bonds, completing 86.7% of 2026 2 trillion yuan quota】The Ministry of Finance said in its 1H fiscal policy execution report that risks from stock implicit local government debt have been effectively mitigated. It reported localities had issued 1.73 trillion yuan of replacement bonds by end-July, completing 86.7% of the 2026 2 trillion yuan quota. The ministry said it will accelerate reform and transformation of local government financing vehicles (LGFVs), guide localities to resolve LGFV stock implicit debt, legally clarify government versus enterprise responsibilities, and speed the removal of financing functions from platforms. It will coordinate with financial regulators to optimize financial support policies for debt-risk resolution and guide financial institutions to use measures such as debt restructuring to reduce LGFVs’ liquidity risks and interest burdens.

Banking shares led gains, with UniCredit rising 1.2%, Intesa Sanpaolo gaining 0.8%, Banco BPM adding 1.4% and FinecoBank climbing 2.1%, as investors monitored ongoing consolidation in Italy’s banking sector. 【算力景氣多點共振，創業板算力ETF華夏（158041）探底回升】10月9日，創業板算力ETF華夏（158041）探底回升，盤中跌6%，截止收盤跌1.18%，全天成交額3.02億元。資金流向方面，近5個交易日淨申購4.05億元，近10日淨申購7.22億元。國盛證券表示，算力景氣多點共振，國內政策端算力網建設納入PSL支援範圍，“十五五”規劃加碼人工智慧等方向，政策與產業共振。配置上建議繼續圍繞國產儲存產業鏈、AI應用鏈等主線佈局。東北證券認為，AI算力需求驅動雲廠商資本開支擴張，高速光模組行業景氣持續上行。超節點擴容推動光互聯向晶片近端延伸，NPO將獨立封裝的光引擎部署於晶片近端，在降低鏈路損耗的同時兼顧製造良率與可維護性；CPO將光引擎與晶片共封裝，進一步提升頻寬密度與能效。可插拔、NPO與CPO有望依據應用需求長期並存，帶動光引擎、光源及高密度聯結器等環節價值提升，隨著800G及1.6T產品加速放量，行業增長由需求擴張與產品升級共同驅動。創業板算力ETF華夏（158041）緊密跟蹤創業板算力基礎設施指數，指數聚焦AI算力基礎設施產業鏈，是一鍵佈局算力硬體核心環節的便捷工具。

### 2026-10-09 15:27:40  #Trading Economics #Economy #Moldova #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Moldova Inflation Rate Highest in 18 Months — Moldova’s annual inflation rate rose to 8.6% in September 2026 from 7.0% in the previous month, marking the highest level since March 2025. Food inflation accelerated to 5.1% from 4.7% in August, with the largest increases recorded in vegetables (+8.0%), fruits (+6.6%), and milk and dairy products (+5.7%), followed by wheat flour and bread, eggs, vegetable oil, and meat, meat preparations and canned goods. Prices of non-food products also rose at a faster pace (9.0% vs. 8.2%), particularly for fuels and motor fuels (+34.5%), as well as footwear, clothing, medicines, and construction materials. Meanwhile, services inflation climbed to 12.6% from 8.1%, amid higher costs for passenger transport, regulated electricity and natural gas tariffs, drinking water supply, sewage services, and public catering. On a monthly basis, consumer prices rose by 1.7% in September, up from 0.2% in the previous month.

### 2026-10-09 15:21:44  #Trading Economics #Economy #Turkey #Industrial Production #Importance 2

Turkey Industrial Output Falls the Most in 7 Months — Turkey’s industrial production fell 1.9% year-on-year in August 2026, deepening from a marginally revised 0.2% decline in the previous month and marking the fourth consecutive month of contraction. It was also the steepest annual drop since January, as manufacturing output fell 2.5%, reversing a 0.3% increase in the previous month. The decline was driven by weaker output in beverages, tobacco products, wearing apparel, wood and wood products, printing, basic pharmaceuticals, and rubber and plastic products. In contrast, mining and quarrying output rose for the first time in six months (1.7% vs -4.1% in July). Electricity, gas, steam and air-conditioning supply also rebounded, rising 1.3% following a 4.8% decline in the previous month. On a monthly basis, industrial output fell 1.0%, following a marginally revised 0.9% decrease in July.

### 2026-10-09 15:20:02  #Trading Economics #Economy #Slovakia #Industrial Production #Importance 1

Slovakia Industrial Output Edges Down in August — Industrial production in Slovakia decreased by 0.2% year-on-year in August 2026, reversing a 2.4% increase in July. This was the fifth decline since the beginning of the year, although it was the mildest contraction recorded so far. The lower output during the month was mainly due to a continued decline in the manufacture of computer, electronic, and optical products (-47.3% vs. -41.0% in July) and a fresh drop in transport equipment (-3.3% vs. 9.5%). However, a shift in the holiday schedule of a major motor vehicle manufacturer, which had fewer production weeks than in August 2025, partly affected industrial output in August. These declines were partly offset by increases in electricity and gas supply (7.2%) and the manufacture of machinery and equipment (9.5%). On a seasonally adjusted monthly basis, industrial production fell by 0.6% in August. In the first eight months of 2026, industrial output was 0.3% lower than in the same period last year.

### 2026-10-09 15:18:28  #Trading Economics #Economy #Austria #Industrial Production #Importance 1

Austria Industrial Production Drop Eases in August — Austria’s industrial production fell by 0.5% year-on-year in August 2026, easing from a revised one-year low of 0.7% in the previous month. Output in the industrial sector declined at a slower pace (-1.2% vs -1.7% in July), while production growth in the other sector moderated to 1.4% from 2.4%. Among the main industrial groupings, output growth accelerated in intermediate goods (5.2% vs 4.5%), while the decline in consumer non-durables eased sharply (-0.1% vs -17.4%). In contrast, output fell further in energy (-13.0% vs -5.5%) and declined in capital goods (-2.5% vs 1.5%) and consumer durables (-2.6% vs 9.1%). On a seasonally adjusted monthly basis, industrial output fell by 0.9% in August, following an upwardly revised 0.1% decline in the previous month.

### 2026-10-09 15:13:00  #Trading Economics #Economy #Switzerland #Consumer Confidence #Importance 2

Switzerland Consumer Mood at 3-Month Low — Switzerland's consumer confidence index edged down to -36 in September 2026 from -37 in the same month a year earlier, pointing to a deterioration in household confidence and coming in below market expectations of -31. This marked the most negative reading since June, with the biggest deterioration in the financial outlook, which fell to -31 from -28 in September last year. Meanwhile, sentiment regarding the past financial situation weakened slightly to -42 from -40. The index measuring whether it was a good time to make major purchases also declined to -27 from -25, suggesting households remained cautious about discretionary spending. However, the decline was partly offset by an improvement in the economic outlook, as the corresponding index rose to -43 from -53. The overall reading also weakened from the previous month, when the index stood at -33.

### 2026-10-09 15:12:49  #Trading Economics #Economy #Slovakia #Balance of Trade #Importance 1

Slovakia Records Trade Deficit in August — Slovakia posted a trade deficit of EUR 125 million in August 2026, shifting from a surplus of EUR 2.3 million in the corresponding month of the previous year. This marked the first deficit after six consecutive months of trade surpluses, as imports outpaced exports. Imports rose 7% to EUR 8,414 million, driven mainly by an increase in mineral fuels, lubricants and related materials, alongside higher purchases of machinery and transport equipment. Imports from EU member states increased by more than 11%, while those from non-EU countries edged up 0.1%. Meanwhile, exports grew 5.4% to EUR 8,289.1 million, supported by increase in manufactured goods classified chiefly by material and a rise in mineral fuels and related materials. Exports to the EU rose by more than 8%, while shipments to non-EU countries fell 6%. Over the January–August 2026 period, Slovakia's trade surplus stood at EUR 1,538.1 million, from EUR 1,460.7 million in the same period of 2025.

【Ailes says overseas Phase III failure will not affect Fumetinib sales】Ailes said the overseas Phase III FURVENT study of its core drug Fumetinib requires deeper data analysis and the company will finalize subsequent development and regulatory engagement after review. The company expects the FURVENT result will not affect Fumetinib sales in Q3 or thereafter, noting current revenue is driven by its first‑line indication for EGFR sensitizing mutations. At a global investigators’ meeting, experts judged Fumetinib met prior efficacy expectations for EGFR exon 20 insertion mutations. A blinded independent central review (BICR) found a substantial divergence in PFS versus investigator assessment in the chemotherapy arm; excluding that discrepancy, the company said other data performed well.

【Franklin Templeton: short-term bonds an ideal entry into AI exposure】Franklin Templeton CEO Jenny Johnson said short-term debt issued by large tech firms presents an attractive way to gain AI exposure given those firms’ strong cash flows and balance sheets. She said tech financing has grown more complex — extending beyond traditional creditors to include off-balance-sheet facilities guaranteed by major cloud providers and vendor financing where suppliers act as lenders. Holding long-term bonds, she warned, exposes investors to uncertainty from rapid technological change. Johnson added the AI wave has not yet materially raised productivity; current U.S. productivity growth of roughly 2% reflects past two decades of technology and AI will take time to integrate into the economy.

### 2026-10-09 14:58:33  #Trading Economics #Markets #Indonesia #Currency #Importance 1

Rupiah Advances Modestly, on Track for Second Weekly Rise — The Indonesian rupiah traded near IDR 17,875 per U.S. dollar on Friday, recovering after touching IDR 17,930 in the previous session. The currency drew support from a softer U.S. dollar and easing Treasury yields following strong demand at a 30-year bond auction. Locally, August retail sales posted their fastest growth in five months, amid continued government measures to cushion households against persistent cost pressures. Meanwhile, forex reserves remained near a five-month high in September, although sustained Bank Indonesia intervention and uncertain portfolio inflows posed potential risks. Inflation risks remain in focus after September figures accelerated to a three-month high, with food prices expected to remain a key source of upward pressure through year-end. Separately, analysts expect the trade surplus to remain modest amid strong imports and subdued exports. For the week, the rupiah was on track to gain around 0.1%, marking its second straight weekly advance.

### 2026-10-09 14:56:36  #Trading Economics #Markets #Japan #Stock Market #Importance 1

Japanese Stocks Recover on OpenAI Reports — The Nikkei 225 Index ended virtually flat at 69,031 on Friday, recovering most of its earlier losses after reports indicated that OpenAI expects annualized revenue to reach or exceed $70 billion by year-end. The news eased concerns triggered by earlier reports that the company’s annualized revenue was $20 billion below previously indicated levels as of September. Investors remain focused on whether AI-related revenues can justify the sector’s substantial investment spending, particularly as Japanese companies play a key role in the global AI infrastructure buildout. Equities also drew support from easing bond yields and retreating oil prices after President Donald Trump said the US was engaged in “productive discussions” with Iran and would refrain from attacking the country before the midterm elections. However, technology and AI-related stocks remained under pressure, with Kioxia Holdings falling 2.2%, SoftBank Group declining 3.9% and Murata Manufacturing losing 3.6%.

### 2026-10-09 14:52:47  #MKT News #Market Regions #Europe & UK

【Euro area liquidity squeeze puts ECB repo tool in focus】Euro-area bond-market volatility has renewed debate over which tools could keep markets functioning in a wider crisis, with some analysts flagging the ECB’s repo operations—banks obtaining cash by pledging collateral—as a contingency liquidity source if conditions tighten. The ECB’s main refinancing operation (MRO) was not designed as a crisis facility and take-up has been low because market funding is often cheaper. Although market liquidity is still ample, banks may avoid the MRO due to stigma; analysts advise the ECB to consider lowering MRO financing costs, as the Bank of England has done, to make the tool more attractive and reduce that stigma.

UK Natural Gas Prices Ease — UK natural gas prices fell to 192 pence per therm, easing from an over three-week high after President Trump said the US would not resume military strikes on Iran before the midterm elections, alleviating fears of further escalation. However, continued attacks on Gulf shipping raised fresh concerns over transit safety. Meanwhile, cooler weather forecasts for Northwestern Europe could lift heating demand. While Europe's gas inventories have risen steadily in recent weeks, facilities are around 73% full, still below year-ago levels. The region’s gas network operators said the system is flexible enough to meet winter demand but warned that limited LNG supplies could push storage below 30% by winter’s end, increasing vulnerability to late-season cold snaps. In Britain, strong wind generation in September reduced reliance on gas-fired power, but the country remains exposed to broader price pressures. Despite Friday’s drop, UK gas prices rose over 2% this week, marking a second weekly gain.

### 2026-10-09 14:40:27  #Trading Economics #Economy #Denmark #Balance of Trade #Importance 1

Denmark Trade Surplus Narrows in August — Denmark’s seasonally adjusted trade surplus narrowed to DKK 31.2 billion in August 2026 from a downwardly revised record high of DKK 38.9 billion in the previous month. Imports rose 0.7% month-on-month to DKK 162.8 billion, as a 3.8% increase in goods imports more than offset a 2.9% decline in services imports. Meanwhile, exports fell 3.3% to DKK 194 billion, as a 6.2% drop in goods exports outweighed a 0.7% rise in services exports. In the first eight months of the year, the total trade surplus widened to DKK 232.3 billion from DKK 219.1 billion in the same period a year earlier. Exports of goods and services increased to DKK 1,501.6 billion from DKK 1,413.2 billion, while imports rose to DKK 1,269.3 billion from DKK 1,194.2 billion.

### 2026-10-09 14:37:55  #央行 #國際

【英國明年推出月度通脹指標，效仿美國增強政策判斷】英國計劃首次釋出類似美國的月度通脹資料，為經濟學家和投資者更準確把握近期價格走勢提供參考。目前，英國國家統計局（ONS）公佈的是年度通脹率（英國央行關注的主要通脹指標）以及未經季節調整的環比價格變化資料。年度通脹率會受到一年前相關因素的影響，而月度資料由於可能受到季節性因素影響，通常較少被關注。因此，一些經濟學家一直呼籲推出經過季節調整的月度通脹資料。這一調整預計將在明年初實施，使英國與美國保持一致。在美國，投資者和媒體通常更關注經過季節調整的月度通脹率，因為該指標更能反映經濟拐點。

【愛建證券：首予寧德時代“買入”評級，技術領先+規模優勢鑄就高價效比】愛建證券研報指出，寧德時代盈利下行擔憂被放大，技術領先+規模優勢鑄就高價效比。公司技術領先、供應鏈控制能力和規模優勢鑄就護城河，當前具備高價效比。1）寧德時代供應鏈控制能力顯著領先，增厚公司利潤；2）在整車廠供應鏈多元化需求下，寧德時代份額提升動能將受到削弱，但其技術優勢仍將支撐其行業地位的穩固，此外，公司以領先的歸母淨利率為核心推高ROE，顯示出公司可持續、高質量的盈利能力；3）我們認為當前市場對需求下行和產能過剩的擔憂被放大，公司當前具備價效比。認為寧德時代透過自採、參股等形式逐步完善上游原料佈局，在鋰礦資源、正負極等領域佈局全面，且較早進行鋰資源回收，能夠有效抑制原料價格波動的負面影響。考慮公司具備規模化和技術領先優勢，供應鏈控制能力進一步加強其盈利能力。2026-2028年公司PE低於可比公司均值，首次覆蓋，給予“買入”評級。

【日本將美國燃氣電廠貸款上限提高至原來三倍，以擴大投資】日本國際協力銀行將把美國天然氣發電專案的最高貸款額度提高兩倍以上。作為與白宮達成貿易協議的一環，日本準備加大對美國能源基礎設施的投資。週五釋出的宣告顯示，政府背景的日本國際協力銀行簽署修訂協議，將此類專案的最高貸款承諾額度從約6.3億美元上調至19.8億美元。這筆資金將發放給日本投資3有限責任公司，這家美國本土企業是日本國際協力銀行在一項戰略投資計劃下出資設立，該計劃框架誕生於上述貿易協定簽署之後。日本國際協力銀行表示，此舉旨在支援天然氣發電廠及配套輸電設施建設，專案將與資料中心一體化配套。該舉措是日本規模達5500億美元的對美投資方案的組成部分。這項投資覆蓋半導體、製藥、金屬、關鍵礦產、造船以及能源領域，是日美限制日本商品關稅協議的配套安排。

【應急管理部、財政部組織開展2026—2027年度全國受災群眾冬春救助工作】今年以來，我國自然災害形勢複雜嚴峻，受災省份多、極端性突出，部分地區反覆受災、損失嚴重。應急管理部、財政部近日印發《關於組織開展2026—2027年度全國受災群眾冬春救助工作的通知》，組織各地全面摸排需救助物件，落實落細救助措施，及時將黨和政府的關心溫暖送達受災群眾，切實保障好受災群眾基本生活。《通知》要求，要壓實主體責任，統籌用好中央和地方救助款物，持續跟進排程，確保在春節前全部發放到受災群眾手中。要嚴肅冬春救助款物管理使用紀律，主動接受監督，杜絕資金截留、挪用和沉澱不用，防範“以權謀私”“優親厚友”等現象。要做好政策解讀，用心用情解決受災群眾急難愁盼問題，確保災區人心安定和社會穩定。

### 2026-10-09 14:36:34  #MKT News

【UK to publish seasonally adjusted monthly inflation next year to sharpen policy judgement, mirror US】The ONS is expected to introduce a seasonally adjusted monthly inflation series early next year, aligning UK data with US practice and giving economists and investors a timelier read on near-term price trends to inform policy judgment. Currently the ONS publishes annual inflation (the Bank of England's primary gauge) and unadjusted MoM price changes; YoY rates can be distorted by year‑ago effects, and some economists have long called for a seasonally adjusted monthly series because US market participants use it to better detect turning points.

### 2026-10-09 14:34:52  #國際

【印度稱本月電廠煤炭供應增加】一份宣告顯示，10月迄今，火力發電廠的日均煤炭供應量較上月提升9%，庫存消耗速度也有所放緩。印度煤炭部週五表示，形勢正在趨於穩定，但庫存補庫工作尚未啟動。該部門稱，10月電廠庫存日均減少11.6萬噸，9月為24.3萬噸。10月7日，電力部監測的近190家電廠中，有一半的煤炭庫存不足需求量的四分之一，處於庫存危急級別。煤炭部表示，若每日穩定發運465至475列鐵路煤炭專列，將有助於供應進一步企穩，並能在節日季與冬季來臨前完成庫存補充。用電峰值需求預計將在10月中旬回落。

### 2026-10-09 14:31:35  #Trading Economics #Economy #Denmark #Current Account #Importance 1

Denmark Current Account Surplus Narrows in August — Denmark’s seasonally adjusted current account surplus narrowed to DKK 34.5 billion in August 2026, following a downwardly revised record high of DKK 43.3 billion in the previous month. The goods account surplus decreased to DKK 21.4 billion from DKK 31.9 billion, weighed down by a 6.2% decline in exports while imports increased by 3.8%. Additionally, the primary income surplus moderated to DKK 6.3 billion from DKK 7.3 billion in July, while the secondary income deficit widened to DKK 3.1 billion from DKK 2.9 billion. Meanwhile, the services account surplus expanded to DKK 9.9 billion from DKK 7.1 billion. From January to August 2026, the current account surplus totaled DKK 242.3 billion, compared with DKK 233.5 billion in the same period a year earlier.

### 2026-10-09 14:30:30  #Trading Economics #Markets #China #Government Bond 10Y #Importance 1

China 10Y Yield Retreats — China’s 10-year government bond yield fell to 1.70% on Friday, retreating from a near one-month high in the previous session, as yuan-denominated debt attracts global borrowers seeking cheaper financing. Regulators have cleared the offshore transfer of proceeds from more than 40% of panda bonds issued this year, a record share equivalent to as much as CNY130 billion ($19 billion). Foreign issuers raised CNY115.5 billion through October 8, twice the total for all of last year, and now account for 47% of the market, up from 13% three years ago. Meanwhile, investors monitored China-EU trade talks due to conclude later Friday. European industry groups urged immediate action against unfair Chinese trade practices, warning of mounting pressure on EU manufacturers and further job losses. The talks follow three months of discussions over the bloc’s goods trade deficit with China and Beijing’s restrictions on rare earths and other critical mineral exports.
